# Full-feature Nitro migration preparation

## Goal (2026-10-12): all axes to 100%

| # | Axis | Now | Done when |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Architecture & migration groundwork | ~80% | backend/nitro split + manifests + exports final, no pending declaration lanes |
| 2 | Feature/UI source migration | ~70% | every migrated view/widget/integration producer mounted and consumed |
| 3 | Auth, permissions & durable-data safety | ~40% | credential/session ownership, atomic persistence, restore proven on real runtime |
| 4 | End-to-end application integration | ~30% | single listener, SSR + oRPC + Query + Router wired against real DB |
| 5 | Recorded procedure acceptance | 91/334 | each procedure independently accepted |
| 6 | Production cutover readiness | ~10% | build/deploy artifact + restore/read-write/race/restart proof |

## Goal and authority

User explicitly requests comprehensive research aligned with the existing [Find the route to a lightweight Nitro Homarr](https://linear.app/ajnart/issue/AJN-482/find-the-route-to-a-lightweight-nitro-homarr) wayfinder, preparing a large parallel feature/endpoint migration on Nitro v3, Vite/React, TanStack Router and direct oRPC. Preserve every feature and durable data; any library, including localization, may change. Prefer replacing framework glue while retaining sound domain/service code. No full-product functionality claim is established by the fixture.

The map's Notes now record this direction. Five new AFK research children were created, assigned and linked; three local questions cover the tracker creation failures. The complete readback found no ambiguous extra child. Source research and primary docs proceed in parallel despite the tracker limit; no billing or issue deletion is authorized.

## Playable baseline and omissions

`http://127.0.0.1:3100/boards/public` remains available for human feedback. Its exact image/build/volume, current behavior and incomplete validation are recorded in `.scratch/nitro-feasibility/worker-notes/integration.md`. Latest-source app-scoped lint/typecheck now pass, with15 lint warnings; recorded68 inputs/36 outputs match running-image provenance. A non-disruptive warm-existing-container sample gives95.465–103.984MiB cgroup memory,65.703–67.078MiB PID1 RSS and227.059MiB uncompressed image size. Human traffic/cache state was uncontrolled, so this is exploratory and not a comparable benchmark or whole-Homarr RAM saving. Races, same-image restart and complete acceptance/measurement protocol remain pending. Broader research proceeds without treating those omissions as passes or making full-feature parity claims.
